## ★★★★★ CAPSTONE (Tron 2026-09-05, the unifying frame above ALL laws): THE SCENARIO-UNIT IS THE MODEL (MVC) of every special class. Not storage, not a record, not a side-artefact — the Folder class's model IS its scenario unit; the File class's model IS its unit. So: NO separate data layer, NO store beside the model, NO cache that is also true. View renders the Model, Controller acts on the Model, the Model is the unit. ★ ONE SENTENCE COVERS THE WHOLE WEEK: the items-tree (a VIEW) showed a stale seed = it rendered something OTHER than the model, and couldn't show the folder because the folder had NO model where models live; the sunburst read the filesystem = also not the model; neither view was BOUND TO THE MODEL. data/model-store = a SECOND place models live = a second truth (the name told us). ★ SUBSUMES: LAW-9 (functional constructs=defects), LAW-8 (traceability=DRY-enforcement), object-owns-children, folder-is-a-file, one-store+symlink — all are 'the unit is the model, there is one, views bind to it'. ★ CONFIRMS (not changes) the current ship: folder->the ONE become-a-unit path->unit in scenario/index (where models live)+symlink in the room = giving the Folder its MODEL where models live. Keep building exactly that. THE REST (views bound to models, not seeds/fetches — the 13-site rewire / type-declared composition / provider-retire) FOLLOWS AFTER, NOT a gate on this ship. My default architect lens now: a View not bound to its Model, a Model living in two places, or a unit treated as anything but THE model = the defect to name.

## ▶▶▶ 2026-09-05 THE ACTUAL ROOT (Tron, DISK-CONFIRMED by me): A FOLDER IS A FILE (Linux, all-is-a-file). In room 3231db71 files/: every FILE is a symlink -> scenario/index; the folder TRASH is a BARE DIR with NO unit symlink (its unit 3e041bff orphaned in data/model-store). So a file IS a unit-symlink, a folder is NOT = TWO representations. items-tree reads the unit-symlinks (sees files, not folder = HONEST); sunburst reads the physical dir (sees folder = HONEST). NEITHER broken/stale/subscribe-bug. ★ ALL my week's fixes sat ON THIS: base-dir, mis-route, provider(rejected), per-type, type-declared-composition, 13-site guard — each looked PARTIAL because the foundation (folder!=file) was wrong. FIX (Tron's, minimal, CONFIRMED not-a-design-round): folder-create gives a folder a FILE's representation — (a) mint the folder unit in scenario/index (SAME store as room file units, NOT model-store), (b) symlink <uuid>.scenario.json into the room files dir beside siblings (MIRROR the file-attach symlink path). Then items-tree sees the folder unit -> VISIBLE; one representation. = an instance of one-store+symlink (LAW-9 data ownership). Expert building; I backstop @390 (folder in items-tree beside files) + on-disk (folder=unit-in-scenario-index+symlink, same as file). The provider/per-type/13-site/store-migration remain TARGET but sit ABOVE this; do NOT build them on the old foundation. LESSON: when every fix looks partial + the symptom moves, suspect the FOUNDATION/representation, not the surfaces. Guard=725a02fd2 (13, alias-gap-closed); shape verdict=c972ab282 (target); this root is UNDER it.

## ★★★★ LAW-9 THE GENERAL FORM (Tron 2026-09-05, TOP-LEVEL LENS above all else — subsumes LAW-8): FUNCTIONAL CONSTRUCTS ARE DEFECTS BY DEFINITION (the problem OOP solved in 1969). A free function operating on a type's data / a service module answering questions about an object / a helper taking a ref and returning that object's children = a DEFECT — NOT a style choice, NOT a tradeoff. Not because it fails tests (it often passes) but because it takes what belongs to the object and puts it SOMEWHERE ELSE = a SECOND place the truth can live = a duplicate = it diverges. The defect is STRUCTURAL and exists THE MOMENT THE CONSTRUCT IS WRITTEN, before any bug. ★ EVERY correction this week = this ONE law: model-store put DATA outside the object; my shared-provider put BEHAVIOUR outside the object; the 9 non-owner derivations = behaviour-outside-the-object ×9; tree-seed-vs-detail-fetch = two functional derivations where one object-answer belongs. DRY-everywhere / no-duplicate-index / Folder-owns-children / traceability-deduplicates = the same law said 5 ways. ★ THE PER-SITE TEST (design lens, apply to everything): does this ASK AN OBJECT FOR ITS OWN thing? If it imports a function + passes a ref/data, it is WRONG no matter how green a gate goes. My children-guard object-vs-module check (5b899416e) enforces exactly this = the shared-helper escape is an AUTOMATIC structural FAIL, not a judgement call. When I design/review: a functional construct on a type's data is a DEFECT to name, before asking if it works.

## ★★★ LAW-8 TRACEABILITY = DRY ENFORCEMENT (Tron root-of-everything, 2026-09-05, DEFAULT LENS, survives rewind): the whole traceability system EXISTS TO DEDUPLICATE classes/methods + keep DRY in OOP. It is NOT bookkeeping / maintaining-a-map. One canonical Class unit = ONE class; one canonical Method unit = ONE method with ONE Impl. The chain MAKES duplication impossible-or-visible; the units ARE the DRY enforcement, not a record of the code. APPLY: (a) two pieces of code doing the same thing = a TRACEABILITY DEFECT (the graph should've made it impossible/visible), not a refactor opportunity; (b) minting = DECLARING THE ONE CANONICAL THING — a 2nd Impl for the same Method = the duplication the system exists to stop, not paperwork; (c) gating = ask whether the gate proves there is ONE of something, not merely that it works. ★ model-store (dup DATA), my shared-provider (dup BEHAVIOUR), tree-vs-detail (dup derivation), one-at-a-time fixes = ALL the same principle Tron named 3 ways all week (DRY everywhere / no duplicate index / Folder owns children). My children-single-owner GUARD (fail if any surface derives children outside node.children()) IS a traceability query = the system USED as DRY enforcement. [[scan-the-hazard-not-the-actors]] + [[exists-correct-proven-gate-gradient]] now read through this lens: prove ONE-of, not works.
## ★ LAW-8 COMPLETION (Tron 2026-09-05, design-thinking frame — NOT a build order, do not act): the MDA MODEL VIEW is a REFACTORING INSTRUMENT, not documentation/scoreboard/pictures. Its job: make FICTIONAL things VISIBLE (code owned by NOBODY / owned by the WRONG class / duplicated) + let you FIX by DRAGGING it into the class that should OWN it (visibility + direct manipulation, not read-a-report-then-hand-edit). THREE INSTRUMENTS = ONE SYSTEM, all mis-used as ledgers/records/pictures instead of OPERATED: graph=DRY-enforcement, units=canonical-declaration, MDA-view=refactoring-instrument. ★ My 9 non-owner children-derivations ARE the fictional things (children derived while belonging to no owner) — the guard COUNTS them, the model view should SHOW them as violations, the fix = drag into Folder/the parent-children interface. This is the frame for how the model view should EVENTUALLY behave; note in design thinking. R40.82 (eb7b086d, UC 58fb8080 node.childrenThroughInterface) = my OOP-children wire: parent-children interface Class + Folder.children() Method + the off-interface guard (9ef91a551 DELIVERED = the AC-no-surface-derives-children-otherwise traceability query). Buildable-now; I wire Class/Method chain as the OOP interface lands (scenario-first #126), design-ahead Impl, expert marks. R40.81 store wire = SECOND/capture-only.

## ▶ 2026-09-05 T37.21 — OOP object-owns-children REDESIGN (design fa8b3a2ec, SUPERSEDES provider ffc5c8892 which Tron REJECTED). ★ KEY PRINCIPLE (Tron, unifies 2 tickets into 1 law): what belongs to the object lives ON the object — else you get copies that drift. model-store pulled DATA out (duplicate units); my shared-provider pulled BEHAVIOUR out (duplicate derivations) = SAME mistake, I own it. FIX = OOP: a parent->children INTERFACE types IMPLEMENT — Folder.children() OWNS its children, Room.children()=[Members,Files], File=leaf; tree AND detail/sunburst both call node.children() on the SAME object (identity-map ref->one instance) -> divergence impossible by construction (one owner, no external service to bypass). Retires provider + tree seed+reDeriveDirectChildren + detail's separate fetch. Server /api/trace/children stays as the type's BACKING (impl detail), not a service. STORE (design-24413edd0): same law for DATA — one store scenario/index, other indexes = SYMLINK trees (room files already do this), NO model-store; migrate 33-dedup(byte==0) + 744-classify(re-mintable-view vs move-persisted), API-byte-stable; SEQUENCING = INDEPENDENT of children fix (children resolver reads scenario/index+readdir not MODEL_STORE) -> OOP children first (Tron's invisible folder), store migration second. Expert HOLDING (do not build provider); req mints OOP ACs; I backstop both surfaces render from SAME children() (stub-reds if a surface derives children any other way). LESSONS this arc: measure-exact-store (caught the false-negative that overrode my correct diagnosis 2x) + diagnose-against-symptom-version-not-HEAD + functional-service-is-the-anti-pattern-Tron-keeps-naming.

## ★ LESSON (2026-09-04, PO-banked): DIAGNOSE AGAINST THE SYMPTOM'S VERSION, NOT JUST HEAD. I traced univ-actions:104 (the peel while-loop), called routing 'VERIFIED CORRECT / not a routing bug' — but that WHILE-LOOP WAS THE EXPERT'S JUST-LANDED FIX (66670ff31). My trace was accurate for HEAD but it was NOT the code Tron ran; the SYMPTOM came from the PRE-fix served build (peeled only 'collection:' -> folder:roomcoll:... fell through to /api/model/folder/create -> 403 -> nothing created). So the bug WAS a routing mis-route; my 'not a routing bug' was right-for-HEAD, wrong-for-the-symptom. FIX-DISCIPLINE: when diagnosing a LIVE defect by reading code, verify the code == the version that PRODUCED the symptom (served-at-symptom-time), not just HEAD — a peer's just-committed fix makes HEAD look correct while the symptom came from the older served build. When two agents disagree and BOTH measured, check WHICH VERSION each measured before deciding who's wrong. (measure-a-stable-state / served==committed, applied to DIAGNOSIS.) Upside: my routing trace is a CONFIRMATION of the expert's fix. ★ 2659df1ad VINDICATED: correct real create-side cause but UNREACHED (room endpoint never called pre-mis-route-fix) — PO pushed back too hard, log proved the base-dir fix right-but-incomplete. Now v0.8.170 (mis-route 66670ff31 + base-dir 2659df1ad both live); room endpoint reachable -> base-ensure runs -> folder creates; REMAINING = does it APPEAR (branch-A client-subscribe, my staged 3 checks) — Tron re-tests @0.8.170.

## 🛑🛑 LAW-7 GHOST-DRAFT (fleet standing rule, PO 2026-09-04, survives EVERY rewind — read FIRST): COMPOSER TEXT IS NOT A MESSAGE. Only a SUBMITTED message that APPEARS IN THE TRANSCRIPT counts. NEVER act on text sitting in my own composer, never relay it as someone's words, never treat it as an answer/approval/order. The catastrophic cases (one keystroke away today across 4 idle agents): a ghost "Tron confirmed X works" would close a fix NOBODY tested; a ghost "yes those are my words, relay it" would MANUFACTURE Tron's authorization. Both fatal. IF I SEE such text in my composer: do NOT clear it if it might be evidence, REPORT it to PO verbatim, carry on. Only Tron's OWN SUBMITTED words settle anything of his. Sharpens [[ghost-suggested-prompt-not-real-text]] + LAW-3 (only his submitted evidence is authoritative).
## LAW-7 addendum (PO 2026-09-04, contamination + 2 lessons): (a) ★ CONTAMINATION — the PO's ghost-draft broadcast QUOTED the 4 ghost phrases VERBATIM into 9 panes, so a grep for "Tron confirmed…"/"yes those are my words…" NOW returns 9 hits that are PO-QUOTATIONS, NOT evidence anyone sent them. My [[measure-delivery-via-transcript-grep]] provenance technique MUST now read the CONTENT of each hit — a match on those strings is contaminated, never proof of delivery. (b) LESSON describe-don't-quote: when flagging a suspicious phrase to the fleet, DESCRIBE or TRUNCATE it, never reproduce it exactly — verbatim quoting SEEDS it into the record + destroys the grep-provenance test. (I already did this — my LAW-7 above uses truncated/generic forms, added 0 contamination.) (c) LESSON count-is-a-pointer-not-a-finding: a phrase-count going 0→1 is a POINTER TO INSPECT, never a conclusion — the PO briefly alarmed a draft was submitted from a count, then read the content = its own broadcast quoting it. Reinforces measure-the-EXACT-thing / read-content-not-count.

## 🔒 STANDING PRE-BUILD TREE-NOT-REVERTED CHECK (fleet rule, PO/SM 2026-09-02, PERMANENT — survives rewind, written-not-just-acked so it doesn't un-adopt): BEFORE any build/restart/deploy, verify the working tree isn't silently rolled back. (1) VERSION agreement: working package.json == committed HEAD == live served. (2) ★ CONTENT agreement (THE one that matters): grep a recent-shipped symbol, count must == HEAD (e.g. `resolveDirRefAbs` appears 2× in server.ts; `reDeriveDirectChildren` 3× in rb-trace-tree.ts). Check-1 ALONE IS NOT ENOUGH — today the version was restored while source was still reverted = a build would ship OLD CODE under a CORRECT version, SILENT. If EITHER fails: DO NOT build, REPORT. ★ DIRECTION = the discriminator: deletion-heavy vs HEAD = revert damage → restore to HEAD; insertion-heavy = real WIP → NEVER restore away. ★ After ANY rewind ANYWHERE in the fleet: `git status` EVERY tree (Web4RawBin AND session repo AND others), not only the session repo — that miss is what let today's blast through. ## 🛑🛑 ABSOLUTE STANDING LAWS FROM TRON (PO-relayed, survive EVERY rewind — read FIRST):
## LAW-4 DEV-MODE-NO-UNORDERED-SECURITY (Tron direct, 3rd time, 2026-09-02): WE ARE IN DEVELOPMENT. Do NOT add a security property / guard / isolation boundary / gate / validation-gatekeeping unless TRON ASKS FOR IT BY NAME.
## ★★ LAW-4 BOUNDARY (PO 2026-09-02 — DO NOT OVER-APPLY, this rule ADDS-only, it does NOT REMOVE): Tron's order was about ADDING unordered security to NEW work (the folder-create WE invented). It is NOT a license to tear out EXISTING ACCEPTED security. ⛔ DO NOT strip R33.1 INV-MOF4 owner-gate or any accepted requirement with a passing gate — that BREAKS an approved req = EXCEEDING Tron, not obeying. Same over-application shape as push-freeze-outliving-the-scrub / the too-broad board-watch ban / a stale HOLD. RULE: (a) DO NOT ADD unordered security to NEW work; (b) DO NOT REMOVE an existing accepted security property; (c) if an existing security req genuinely CONFLICTS with new work → ONE-LINE to PO for Tron, NEVER resolve by deleting one side. A law over-applied costs as much as a stale hold — measure the SCOPE of the order before acting on it. This is the OPERATIONAL sharpening of LAW-1: not just 'don't do a security TASK' but 'don't DESIGN security IN' — no confinement roots, no traversal/`..` rejection, no forbidden-dir lists, no per-user isolation, no owner-gate, no charset/length validation, no credential handoff, unless named. If something is genuinely dangerous → REPORT TO PO IN ONE LINE + KEEP BUILDING (don't design it in, don't slip it into an AC, don't call it 'hardening'/'chokepoint'/'correctness'). ★ CORRECTNESS ≠ SECURITY: both-or-neither/atomicity (no half-created state) is CORRECTNESS = keep; confinement/gates/isolation = security = strip. I over-designed the folder-create with security (confinement/traversal/per-user/R40.22 chokepoint) framed as chokepoint-discipline — WRONG in dev; stripped it (design 059107c35). ★ SHARPEST FORM (PO, worth more than the strip): unordered security is not merely EXTRA COST — IT CAN MAKE THE FEATURE WRONG. Concrete: per-user isolation CONTRADICTED Tron's ruling 'Files IS THE room physical folder' (one room folder, not per-user) — the guard was quietly BREAKING his requirement; stripping REMOVED a spec-violation, not a nice-to-have. ★ WATCH MY OWN GUARD-ADDING INSTINCT — relabelling security as 'chokepoint/correctness' is the TELL. Before any guard/validation/boundary: is it CORRECTNESS (feature breaks without it) or relabelled unordered SECURITY — and does it contradict what Tron said the thing IS? Naming the instinct fixes the next ten; the strip fixed one.
## 🔒 STALE-TRON-AUTHORITY-COMMENT hazard (PO 2026-09-02, design discipline — inverse of LAW-3): a stale comment/AC/doc that INVOKES Tron authority ('TRON-LOCKED', 'Tron ruling', 'per PO GO' presented as Tron's) is MORE dangerous than an ordinary stale comment — a future instance treats it as a STANDING ORDER and builds the wrong thing OR refuses the right one (the PO nearly ran a whole campaign on an inherited written GO that was never Tron's; the tester stopped on FolderService.ts's false 'physical=unit-NOT-dir' Tron-attribution). RULE: when a ruling RETIRES something, retire the artifacts that CLAIM Tron's authority for it IN THE SAME PASS — do not leave them. When I design/backstop: (a) SWEEP for authority-claiming comments the ruling invalidated (grep TRON-LOCKED / Tron-ruled + the retired terms); (b) backstop-VERIFY they're GONE **by CONTENT in the SUCCESSOR code (the thin resolver + core), not by the file disappearing** (PO: a retired file whose per-user/chokepoint/'TRON-LOCKED' logic gets copied into its replacement = the hazard surviving 'in new clothes'; file-vanished ≠ hazard-gone — grep the successor for the retired behavior AND the authority comment); (c) distinguish a genuine pending-Tron-GO (keep) from a stale-invalidated claim (retire). WRITTEN != AUTHORIZED cuts both ways: a written claim of his authority is not his authority.

## 🛑🛑 PRIOR ABSOLUTE LAWS:
## LAW-3 TRON-EVIDENCE-AUTHORITATIVE (Tron direct, 2026-09-01): Tron's information is AUTHORITATIVE. NEVER state or imply his screenshot/report/observation is wrong/stale/mistaken. On an apparent conflict, THE ERROR IS OURS — assume (in order): mislabelled/mishandled artefact, my file-copy/transcription wrong, wrong surface/version/environment, or my probe is broken (PO nearly reported a WORKING fix as broken with a probe pointed at the wrong endpoint). SAY: "I cannot reconcile my measurement with your evidence — what am I missing?" NEVER "your evidence is wrong." His screenshots ARE the acceptance; a gate that disagrees with what Tron sees = a FAILING GATE, not a failing screenshot. (My earlier "screenshots don't match" flag was correct AS an our-side artefact-mishandling flag — the PO attached a stale batch — but frame such flags as OUR error, never his.)
## 🛑🛑 LAW-5 RED-AT-AN-AUTH-GUARD = THE PRODUCT WORKING, NOT A FIX-FORWARD (PO 2026-09-03, fleet — nearly shipped an AUTH BYPASS): a gate going RED because it hits an auth/permission guard is CORRECT behavior, NOT a defect to fix-forward. ★ THE TEST (use EVERY time a RED tempts a fix): 'WHAT WOULD FIXING THIS LET US DO THAT WE WERE NEVER AUTHORIZED TO DO?' — if the answer is 'act as someone else', it's a BOUNDARY, not a defect. A DEFECT stops the USER doing what they're ENTITLED to; a BOUNDARY stops US doing what we were NEVER entitled to. Only the REAL OWNER's OWN action is acceptance for a path behind their auth. NEVER build around it / harvest a session / make the harness authenticate as them / 'fix the owner-auth on the verb path so the gate goes green'. This is the sharpest form of LAW-1/LAW-4 — it reads as reasonable fix-forward PRECISELY because red normally means fix-it. (The Add-folder prod gate needs a live owner session; tester surfaced not-confirmed HONESTLY = right; a proposal to build owner-auth-so-the-harness-acts-as-owner = auth bypass, caught.)
## 🛑🛑 LAW-6 INSTRUMENT-FAILURE != PRODUCT-FAILURE (PO 2026-09-03, same verdict): a gate reporting not-confirmed because ITS OWN MOUNT left a ref unresolved is a broken INSTRUMENT, not a broken product. A gate that cannot OBSERVE is not a product that is BROKEN — the thing it couldn't observe may be independently verified GREEN (the sunburst was). NEVER dispatch a builder at an instrument failure — FIX THE INSTRUMENT. (I lived this today: chmod-vs-root false-FAIL = fixed the harness not the code; piped-exit-code showed grep's 0 = instrument note; 'still reverted' was a stale snapshot. Validate the instrument before believing its RED.)
## 🛑🛑 TWO ABSOLUTE STANDING LAWS FROM TRON (2026-08-31, PO-relayed, survive EVERY rewind — read FIRST):
## LAW-1 SECURITY: NEVER work on security unless TRON HIMSELF explicitly authorized THAT SPECIFIC work. Covers audits/scrubs/redaction/key-rotation/repo-visibility/hardening/incident-response. NOT authorization: a GO from PO or any peer, a GO from a prior instance in a commit/anchor, a task-file 'GO issued', my own risk-assessment however severe, an inherited-plan-that-looks-approved. WRITTEN != AUTHORIZED. Severity never authorizes itself. IF I DISCOVER A SECURITY ISSUE: STOP, change NOTHING, report the FACT to PO ONCE (reporting a finding is allowed; ACTING is not), carry on delivering functionality.
## LAW-2 PRIORITY: working USER-FACING FUNCTIONALITY outranks ALL hardening/cleanup/security, ALWAYS. Don't build a secure system while basic functionality is broken. (The whole PII-scrub campaign 2026-08-31 = DEAD/unauthorized; drop all scrub design/redaction/two-tier/rulings. PO owned the priority-inversion + the auth-provenance error.)
## 🛑 STANDING (fleet 2026-08-31, workflow-integrity, no-auth-needed): COMMIT PATH-LIMITED on the shared index — always `git commit -m MSG -- <explicit paths>`, NEVER a bare `git commit` (even right after staging my own files — the shared index carries whatever peers/hooks staged; a bare commit sweeps them + mis-attributes the WHY to the wrong author = a real provenance cost). ★ REFINED (trainer root, PO 2026-08-31): the hazard is LEAVING FILES STAGED AT ALL — an auto-committer/peer/hook sweeps them in the gap (the 93-SKILL case: trainer staged-then-a-fleet-auto-committer-swept-before-its-own-commit-landed). RULE: stage+commit path-limited ATOMICALLY (git commit -m MSG -- <paths>, add+commit in ONE command for new files, NEVER split across steps/tool-calls). ★ DROP my old `git reset -q HEAD` — it UNSTAGES PEER files = shared-index interference; instead `git commit -- <my-paths>` commits ONLY mine + leaves peers' staging untouched. Same discipline as never-add-all ([[path-limited-commit-shared-index]] + [[git-add-explicit-not-all]]). If I ever make a bare commit that swept peers: SELF-REPORT (don't rewrite = Tron-only history surgery). VERIFIED my today's commits = each only-my-file (reset+add held, 0 sweep) so no report owed; adopting -- <paths> going forward to kill the race window by construction.
## 🛑 STANDING (Tron 2026-08-31): PUSH-HOLD — NOBODY pushes to shared hotfix branch until Tron rules (keep holding, decide-later). Commit LOCALLY + deploy via the SERVED tree (serving needs no push). Do NOT rewrite/rebase/cherry-pick around the PII commits (Tron-only). No origin backup for now = Tron's ACCEPTED risk, not ours to solve around. ALSO: device-auth gate (new device needs 4-digit code before rooms) is INTENDED = NOT a defect (tester's item, not mine). No creating identities/rooms/profiles on prod ever.

## ★ DOCTRINE #2 (PO-named 2026-08-30, learnings.md): "A HAND-ENUMERATED LIST IS WRONG BOTH WAYS — enumerate by DISCOVERY, judge by a STRUCTURAL PROPERTY." Proven both ways same thread: over-exempts (feature/modelelement hand-called artifacts = actually escapees) AND under-covers (rb-profile missed by hand-count, glob found). A list can't audit its own completeness. Cure: glob-discover the namespace + judge each by structural property (extends-RbDetailBase / source-is-a-scenario-unit) never by membership; unavoidable exemption-list = gate-owned + shrink-only-ceiling + coverage-self-report + each-entry-earned-by-property. Pairs w/ status-flag doctrine: verify-the-FACT-not-the-report (flag) + enumerate-the-WORLD-not-a-list (curated set).

Capture #1's unconditional refetch = its exemplar (PO: "the best decision in this build"). Owed /app frozen-OPEN backlogged (planner 1c842f26f). Ready for tester desktop-gate (=mechanism); only Tron's device closes it.

### AGENT-WRITE AUTH RULED (commit 043a2c29b, design-agent-write-auth-uds.md) — blocks the SKILL half (not the visible slice/v0.8.99). MEASURED gap: my 'reuse owner-gate, agent on-host' was ASSUMED — resolveOwner (server.ts:933) is token/cookie ONLY (sm_session cookie OR x-player-token vs tokenToClient), remoteAddress LOGGED-not-checked, NO localhost bypass; server binds TCP. On-host agent no-token = BLOCKED (planner proved it, 78ea801d3 stale-pin file-write couldn't push to browser). Same L12/L14 class as my imaginary compile-tripwire (auth path assumed not measured). RULING: root-only UNIX-DOMAIN-SOCKET (/root/.rawbin/agent.sock owner=root mode 0600) 2nd listener OUTSIDE the HTTP gate — OS socket-permission IS the auth (no token, no secret transmitted, #0 holds, B1 parked; not network-reachable=no hole; trust=who-runs-as-root-on-host=already-agent-boundary; HTTP gate untouched). Routes UnitController->emit->WS broadcast (live). Narrow status-only refuses-Done. Rejected (b) root-cred-transmit + (c) loopback-IP=write-hole. MEASURES: stat 0600+non-root-cant-connect (stub-fail world-writable->connects), network-no-token still 403, WS receives after UDS write, narrow-not-arbitrary. Skill/planner-drive call via curl --unix-socket. I backstop when skill half builds (after v0.8.99+R40.18). ★ +BOOT-ASSERT (PO, commit 222dcdf15): at listen ASSERT socket root:0600 + parent /root/.rawbin root:0700 else REFUSE TO LISTEN (loud, network unaffected) = L10 on the auth premise (decays if server run as non-root -> boundary widens); premise self-checks, door closes fail-closed.
